... we're stopped by this one man on a bridge between us and the Temple.
I thought I'd finally found a way to even things out by resonating with the Golden Bough, but...
... I just don't see how we can get past this wall.
**Jia Qiu:** Do not kneel.
A low, deep voice boomed from behind us.
**Lei Heng:** Well I'll be— Ya sure got here fast.
**Lei Heng:** You coulda just taken yer sweet darn time, but you just had to get here now right as I was 'bout to finish 'em off, eh?
**Lei Heng:** Ohhh~! I see now. Honey bunny didn't dig that hole for no reason, huh.
**Lei Heng:** I'm mighty honored you came all the way down here to see me, but I reckon things'll go south real quick without ya bustin' heads out there.
**Jia Qiu:** My presence here took precedence.
**Jia Qiu:** For they are not the kind of people who must stagnate here.
**Lei Heng:** That so?
**Lei Heng:** Hwell, lessee, then.
**Lei Heng:** Let's see who'll greet them fellers when they're done with their business...
**Lei Heng:** Let's see which of us'll get to roll out the red carpet.
**Jia Qiu:** …….
**Lei Heng:** Always wanted to kick yer behind. Ain't this a mighty fine place to do it?
**Lei Heng:** Hot dang, I'm burnin' up...!
Despite Lei Heng's gung-ho provocations, Jia Qiu paid him no mind. He looked to Zilu, who was barely breathing. She was slumped against the wall.
**Jia Qiu:** How long will you remain in slumber, Zilu?
**Lei Heng:** She ain't gonna wake up anytime soon, friendo. I really cut 'er up good this time.
**Lei Heng:** Why don't I put ya to rest right next to 'er?
Lei Heng's blade flashed with fire once again, and he charged like a lightning strike toward Jia Qiu.
But...
Jia Qiu's staff brought Lei Heng's blade to a screeching halt.
**Hong Lu:** Brother...
**Jia Qiu:** Go.
**Jia Qiu:** Lei Heng's forces have already been alerted to the fact that Jia Xichun has already entered the Tiekan Temple.
**Jia Qiu:** Thus... in that brief moment where Jia Xichun completes her ascension and exits the Tiekan Temple...
**Jia Qiu:** ... they will attempt to cut off her head before she gains full control of the Hierarch's Heishou Pack.
**Jia Qiu:** They will use the loophole that the ritual to become the Family Hierarch isn't truly over until she rises to the surface.
**Jia Qiu:** Jia Xichun does not have any substantial foundation nor sponsor who protects her—no family will come to her aid; it is most likely that they are simply waiting for someone to kill her in their stead.
**Jia Qiu:** However...
**Jia Qiu:** One thing I can give you is that, once you enter, and until you exit the temple alongside Jia Xichun...
**Jia Qiu:** ... not a single foe shall cross this bridge as long as I stand. Jia Xichun will survive this day.
**Hong Lu:** And we will bring Xichun safely back to the surface and escort her to the seat of the Family Hierarch.
**Jia Qiu:** Indeed. It will be a considerable undertaking for any family to strike down a Family Hierarch, even one without a foundation like Xichun.
**Lin Daiyu:** ... If that is your will, then I will help as well.
**Hong Lu:** Daiyu...? How did you...
**Lei Heng:** What in the goshdarn... I thought the Xue family'd turned against Daguanyuan already.
**Lin Daiyu:** ... Not all Xue has.
**Lin Daiyu:** Miss Baochai has sent me in secret to assist you.
**Hong Lu:** …….
**Lin Daiyu:** I have not come to discuss or pursue a lofty goal like... the future of Daguanyuan.
**Lin Daiyu:** I only wish that— ... Neither I nor Miss Baochai wish to see you fall here.
**Lin Daiyu:** ... That is all.
**Jia Qiu:** ... Then stand with me.
**Jia Qiu:** Stand with me against the tide and defeat any foe that has made it through.
**Lin Daiyu:** Gladly.
**Hong Lu:** ... Thank you, Daiyu.
**Outis:** …!
**Outis:** Over there, soldiers...! A different force is marching toward the temple.
**Lei Heng:** You knew darn well that this was only a matter of time.
**Lei Heng:** If y'all think ya got what it takes, just try 'n stop us. Lessee if y'all can live up to your boasts.
**Jia Qiu:** Go forth, Jia Baoyu.
**Jia Qiu:** I have faith that your heart is true.

#### Tiekan Temple Entrance

With them defending the bridge, we managed to reach Tiekan Temple's entrance...
... only to find the last people we ever wanted to see waiting at its gates.
**Dante:** N Corp.... And their troops, all of them wearing E.G.O Gear...
It almost looks like they came here ready for a war.
**Jia Huan:** It has happened exactly as the Director said so herself, Gubo.
**Yi Sang:** … Goodness.
**Gubo:** What brings you such distress, my wilted and fallen, old fellow?
**Gubo:** It would have been no trouble for someone of your intelligence to surmise that we would be waiting where your journey ends.
**Gubo:** Or... has your love for the company of these new fellows slowed your mind?
**Yi Sang:** The path I walk is a busy one.
**Yi Sang:** ... And I have no words or sentiments to impart to someone such as yourself, when a dear fellow of mine requires my support and haste.
**Gubo:** ... Huh.
Gubo opened his mouth to say something in response, but Yi Sang swiftly brushed past him without hesitation.
And...
**Jia Huan:** You certainly took your time, little brother.
**Gregor:** What's he...?
**Jia Huan:** Just waiting out here to greet you and your friends.
**Jia Huan:** Do not glare at me so—you need only accept our offer, and we shan't have to fight.
**Jia Huan:** A... temporary truce, if you will.
**Ishmael:** ... Seriously, a truce? Here and now?
**Jia Huan:** It so happens that we have business to see to in there.
**Jia Huan:** ... And Jia Baoyu... you're our only key.
**Jia Huan:** You must've come here in search of the Golden Bough, as you always are.
**Jia Huan:** But that is not what we've come here for.
**Gubo:** We intend to leave here as soon as we earn the 'sample', something far more important to us than the Golden Bough itself.
**Gubo:** Let us not engage in a skirmish that, after all, shall be in vain.
**Sinclair:** And you just expect us to believe—
**Jia Huan:** Think about it for a second. Would we have forfeited with so little hesitation, had our purpose been to claim the Golden Bough?
**Jia Huan:** We thought coming in third would be more than enough to procure what we came for.
**Ishmael:** I see... So you weren't acting on behalf of the Jia family at all... but for N Corp. all along.
**Jia Huan:** ... Are you all really that stupid? You must be.
**Jia Huan:** I don't recall ever hiding my allegiance to N Corp. the last time we met... or did all of you collectively choose to forget that?
**Jia Huan:** Besides... I wouldn't lower myself to participate in this messy little game of Family Hierarch war for something so worthless as the honor of the Jia family.
**Jia Huan:** I have not once lived for this family since the day I changed my name.
**Hong Lu:** …….
**Jia Huan:** ... The Shi family hired us, the N Corp. New League of Nine Littérateurs.
**Jia Huan:** Our job was to void the results of the Evaluation if a Shi family candidate fails to win the contest.
**Jia Huan:** They never change, do they? Mediocre qualifications, yet... always coveting something far beyond their reach.
**Jia Huan:** And as you might expect, N Corp. does not give up so easily.
**Jia Huan:** If you would not accept my truce, we can simply... sit here and wait for you to do something in this standoff. We're not the ones pressed for time.
**Outis:** ... I hope we're not taking his words at face value.
**Gregor:** We're not, but... do we have any alternatives? He's already stalled us enough.
**Sinclair:** He's right, we're the ones running out of time...
**Sinclair:** Jia Qiu is strong, really strong, but... not even he can stop all those soldiers on his own forever.
I'm growing anxious.
He says he's willing to leave us alone once he gets what he wants, but... there's no way for us to be sure of that.
But it's hardly a good idea to fight them now or remain locked in this standoff for who knows how long.
**Dante:** If this fight is inevitable... it's better to enter a truce with them for now, find Xichun, and then fight them if we must.
Besides, Hong Lu said Xichun might be in danger. I had to prioritize that, no matter what.
**Dante:** ... It can't be helped. Let's head inside.
So I made the call.
**Hong Lu:** Okay, let's try your idea.
**Jia Huan:** So you've decided to open the door? Hmph, at least your leader has some semblance of intelligence.
**Jia Huan:** Here's to our fruitful yet brief cooperation.
**Jia Huan:** Lead the way, we'll be right behind you.
And we...
... finally made it to the great gates of Tiekan Temple.
**Dante:** Hong Lu...?
Hong Lu approached the gate handles.
Then, with one hand over his other eye, he brings his jade eye close to an engraved hole in the gates.
**Hong Lu:** My eye is the only key... to opening these gates from the outside.
**Hong Lu:** It's so that I can visit the elders whenever they want me there.

**Kong Qiu:** Our task is not over until she ascends to the seat of the Family Hierarch and seizes the Heishou Packs' leash.
Jia Qiu's resolute voice woke us from that stunned silence, and...
... with him at the vanguard, one by one, we routed the forces that still sought to stop the new Hierarch from assuming the responsibility.
It was no easy task, but...
**Hong Lu:** …….
... Hong Lu appeared more unburdened than ever, so...
... I was pretty happy.
... A few days later...

#### Daguanyuan Front Gates

**Jia Xichun** (*The Hierarch of Hongyuan*): …….
**Hong Lu:** Here to see us go, Xichun?
... She doesn't seem too happy for this to be just a friendly gesture.
**Sinclair:** Hey... we'll stop by from time to time.
**Jia Xichun:** Hmph, sure. But don't expect me to make the time to greet you guys.
**Jia Xichun:** I'm about to be busier than I've ever been in my entire life.
**Wei** (*Xichun's Clan*): My lady expresses that you will always be welcomed with the greatest honors here.
**Sinclair:** Huh... I don't really know you well, Mister Wei, but... I feel a strange sense that we might be kindred spirits...
**Rodion:** Looks like you'll have your hands full too, Wei.
**Wei:** Indeed I will. There is much work to be done.
**Wei:** So much of the information has been esoterically handed down via the Anamnaworm... it'll take years for us to put it all to paper. We're having difficulty even getting an idea of how long it might take.
**Wei:** But you have broken one of many cycles here; I am certain that Hongyuan will become a better place to live under my ladyship's rule.
A lot of things did not go according to plan, but... in the end, Xichun did become the next Hierarch.
A Family Hierarch without the Imperial Worm.
**Jia Xichun:** Everyone here at Daguanyuan, they... they all seem to have a lot of questions for me, but...
**Jia Xichun:** Heh, what are they gonna do? Directly challenge the Family Hierarch?
**Rodion:** Gotta say, ya were real charismatic out there from your first command as the Hierarch~ "Turmoil has come to Daguanyuan. Release the Heishou Pack - Chou, Chen, and Shen branches and quell the chaos!" Right?
**Jia Xichun:** ... Don't *ever* do an impression of me acting like that again.
**Yi Sang:** Many must be looking forward to your governance, given your initial charismatic demonstration.
**Yi Sang:** You are, in essence, a new kind of leader that this land hasn't seen in many years... Much they must have to ask for your judgment in terms of rules and laws.
**Jia Xichun:** Yeah, what about it? Rules are there to be rewritten.
**Jia Xichun:** ... The whole... damn thing, I guess...
Jia Xichun sighed lightly and looked around Daguanyuan.
**Jia Xichun:** Ugh... I feel like I've aged ten years all at once.
**Zigong** (*Qiu's Clan*): I thought I'd be the artist behind the brush for this painting, yet...
**Zigong:** ... I did not expect us to allow a brush held by another to guide us, my lord.
**Kong Qiu:** …….
**Zigong:** It is fortunate that our brushstrokes shared the general direction.
**Zigong:** A Family Hierarch who does not seek to be free from aging and death... I wonder what winds of change that will bring to Hongyuan?
**Kong Qiu:** It was a far more peaceful resolution than the one your guidance would have provided. "Gouge out your little brother's eye and use it to enter the temple", was it?
**Rodion:** Whaaat?!
**Ryōshū:** Efficiency in the veil of cruelty. Minimalistic... puff.
**Zigong:** It was a mere jest, my lord. Though... I suppose I did add "If things do not go according to plan...".
**Zigong:** Ah, whichever was the case... What will you do next, my lord?
**Kong Qiu:** ... Today, I observed that the Pinky has strayed from the path of humanity. Thus, I will now join Yan Hui, journey to Mount Liang, and come to a few agreements with the Mohists.
**Zilu** (*The Pinky*): We must also discuss the matter of the Tiantui Star, whose seat has just become vacant.
**Kong Qiu:** Indeed we must. Then we shall journey to Q Corp. next.
**Dante:** The Pinky?
**Faust:** Yes. ... It seems that Kong Qiu's allegiance lies with the Pinky.
**Kong Qiu:** Indeed. As one of the Pinky, I intend to change the world with the Way of true humanity.
**Sinclair:** Given what Ryōshū said...
**Sinclair:** Lei Heng and Zilu are both from the Pinky, right...? So how come they were trying to kill each other?
**Faust:** Members of the Pinky gather and scatter when certain conditions are met. However, when there is no call...
**Ryōshū:** ... they just live their lives without knowing which is which. No call, no solidarity.
**Ryōshū:** P.O.S.
**Sinclair:** Playing on Solo-mode... right?
**Faust:** It seemed that Zilu and Lei Heng were aware of each other's identities, but did not know about Kong Qiu.
**Zigong:** You know this well. I hear that you are the primary purveyor of esoteric knowledge in your group?
**Faust:** …….
**Zigong** (*The Pinky*): Then, you must also be aware of the weight of information regarding our, and our lord's allegiance to the Pinky is rather heavy.
**Zigong:** I trust that you will maintain your silence regarding the matter, as it is right to do so.
**Kong Qiu:** I have aught to ask you.
Kong Qiu approached me as the other people were starting to scatter.
At first, I looked away because I couldn't imagine that he could possibly be talking to me.
**Kong Qiu:** Dante, the manager.
**Kong Qiu:** Will you continue to push to open their paths...
**Kong Qiu:** ... for every Sinner under your management, no matter which road they walk?
**Kong Qiu:** As you have done for my brother?
I nodded.
**Kong Qiu:** Even if one of them seeks to walk the path of Asura?
Kong Qiu's gaze briefly lingered on one of the Sinners before moving on.
The Sinner takes a long draw from the cigarette and releases the smoke with a large sigh, which then turns into a scattering plume. Her hand rests, out of habit, on her never-drawn sword.
I'm going to keep helping them as long as they have the will to move forward, no matter what. My thoughts on that matter haven't changed at all. They never will.
I nodded once again.
**Kong Qiu:** ... I see.
**Zigong:** ... Ah. My lord, I have another subject to discuss. While this is but a conjecture at the moment... evidence seems to point to Jia Yuanchun being among the treasonous forces of Hongyuan, in cahoots with the Xue and the Shi families.
**Kong Qiu:** While this suspicion is yet to be developed beyond supposition, it seemed likely that Jia Yuanchun had aligned herself with the treasonous forces of Hongyuan.
**Kong Qiu:** She made herself scarce as soon as the troubles were quelled, which could mean... yes.
**Zigong:** The first thread to find is the influential individual within the Shi family who ordered Shi Yihua's execution. Once their identity is determined, it should be no trouble making the connection to the masterminds behind this incident.
**Hong Lu:** Will you continue to roam the lands, brother?
**Kong Qiu:** Indeed I will.
**Kong Qiu:** I will walk the City, converse with the lords of each Nest.
He gives a muted reply to Hong Lu before turning around to look at Jia Xichun.
**Kong Qiu:** ... None may say for certain if Jia Xichun, over her long tenure to come, shall not be tempted by promises of immortality.
**Jia Xichun:** Ugh... seriously?! I'm—
**Kong Qiu:** Yet, if one's heart truly lies steady upon the path of humanity, they will not be swayed to commit evil...
**Kong Qiu:** ... thus, I believe that you are worth watching.
**Kong Qiu:** Upon our next meeting...
**Kong Qiu:** Hm, though I cannot say for certain whether our goals shall be aligned then as they were today, but...
**Kong Qiu:** ... I suppose we shall have much to take in from each other.
**Kong Qiu:** What are your thoughts, Zigong?
**Kong Qiu:** Did you enjoy this lesson as well?
**Zigong:** Yes... I was... foolish, my lord. Once again.
**Kong Qiu:** It is all right.
**Kong Qiu:** Learning and understanding must go hand-in-hand, as I always say.
**Kong Qiu:** You are a student worthy of discourse still.
**Zigong:** ... I have much to learn.
**Zigong:** It seems that you weren't repeatedly recounting that story over and over at me again for nothing.
**Zigong:** You were trying to insinuate that he was the only one in Hongyuan who was worthy of faith...
**Kong Qiu:** ... Zigong, Zilu, let us converse in private for a moment, for I have an important task for you...

#### Daguanyuan Gardens

**Xiren:** …….
Xiren's expression that regarded us was the same as it always was.
**Hong Lu:** Your nu'er tea is always so delicious.
**Hong Lu:** Thanks, Xiren.
**Xiren:** Yes, young master.
**Hong Lu:** …….
**Hong Lu:** I will be departing again soon. I don't know when I should return.
**Xiren:** Understood.
**Hong Lu:** ... Stay safe until then.
**Hong Lu:** A-actually, no.
**Hong Lu:** What do you think, Xiren? About the fact that I'll be leaving soon.
**Xiren:** …….
**Xiren:** Walk the path of your choosing, young master. Whichever that may be.
Her reply sounded as cold as ever, to the point that the now lukewarm, clacking tea cups on her clean-up tray sounded warmer...
**Hong Lu:** Alright.
... but Hong Lu smiled as though something had been lifted from his heart, as though he found something he'd been looking for.

#### Daguanyuan Pavilion

**Lin Daiyu:** Even the opulence of Daguanyuan fades.
**Lin Daiyu:** The trees are wilting... as the once beautiful pavilions and towers now lay in ruins and abandonment.
**Lin Daiyu:** It seems that all of existence is, indeed, but a great cycle of rises and falls.
**Hong Lu:** It is.
**Hong Lu:** But isn't that...
**Hong Lu:** ... exactly why every moment in life is so beautiful?
**Lin Daiyu:** ... Yes, yes it is.
**Lin Daiyu:** …….
**Lin Daiyu:** I will no longer wait for you.
**Lin Daiyu:** And I might one day disappear from Daguanyuan myself, now that I know that idle waiting is not the sole answer.
**Hong Lu:** …….
**Hong Lu:** Daiyu, I loved that day when we went out kiting together.
**Hong Lu:** That was my favorite game.
**Lin Daiyu:** ... And mine as well.
Hong Lu leaves Daguanyuan as the happiest descendant it has ever produced.
Finding meaning in every moment of his life...
No longer a passive witness to tragedy, as he once was to the deaths and suffering of his siblings,
Hong Lu will walk tall with his friends.
Yet even that will one day fade. For in the end, we have but one chance at life, and to live at all is to exist in futility.
His home, too, shall one day be taken by loneliness and solitude, gradually eroding away with time...
and we will all one day go our separate ways.
But every wilting fertilizes the next blossom, every fallen home may become a place for new beginnings, and every goodbye is a promise of new meetings.
Thus Jia Baoyu, once the peerless treasure of Daguanyuan...
... boards the bus once again as Hong Lu.
The clouds I once saw in his jade-eye had all but withdrawn.
I only see an incandescent, radiant smile.
**Jia Xichun:** Well...
